## Administrative Monetary Penalties and Notices (CSA 2001) Regulations {#administrative-monetary-penalties-and-notices-csa-2001-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2008-97
:::

[CANADA SHIPPING ACT, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15)

Registration 2008-04-03

Administrative Monetary Penalties and Notices (CSA 2001) Regulations

P.C. 2008-610 2008-04-03
::::

::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Transport, Infrastructure and Communities, pursuant to
paragraphs 244(f) to (j)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_SOR-2008-97_e_hq_4304){#fn_SOR-2008-97_e_hq_4304-ID0EGCAA}
of the [Canada Shipping Act, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_SOR-2008-97_e_hq_4305){#fn_SOR-2008-97_e_hq_4305-ID0EDCAA},
hereby makes the annexed Administrative Monetary Penalties Regulations.

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_SOR-2008-97_e_hq_4304-ID0EGCAA){#fn_SOR-2008-97_e_hq_4304}S.C. 2001. c.
  29, par. 72(p)
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_SOR-2008-97_e_hq_4305-ID0EDCAA){#fn_SOR-2008-97_e_hq_4305}S.C.
  2001, c. 26
  :::
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-748147 .Part}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,
[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canada Shipping Act,
2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15).

## [Violations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-748149 .Part}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  contravention of a provision of the Act, or of a regulation made under
  the Act, set out in column 1 of a Part of the schedule, or the
  contravention of a direction given under such a provision, is
  designated as a violation that may be proceeded with in accordance
  with sections 229 to 242 of the Act and by the issuance of a notice of
  violation.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The range of penalties set out in column 2 of a Part
  of the schedule is the range of penalties in respect of a violation
  set out in column 1.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "X" is set out in column 3 of a Part of the
  schedule, a violation set out in column 1 constitutes a separate
  violation for each day on which it is continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-246, s. 2
- SOR/2018-86, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2-20121123.html "Link to previous version of section 2"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Service of Documents]{.HTitleText1} {#h-748156 .Part}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section applies in respect of the following documents:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice of violation referred to in paragraph
    229(1)(b) of the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a notice of compliance referred to in section 231
    of the Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a notice of default referred to in subsection
    231.1(1) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document may be served on an individual

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} personally, by leaving a copy of it

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with the individual, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the individual cannot conveniently be found,
      with someone who appears to be an adult member of the same
      household at the last known address or usual place of residence of
      the individual; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by sending a copy of it by registered mail,
    courier, fax or other electronic means to the last known address or
    usual place of residence of the individual.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A document may be served on a corporation by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy of it by fax, registered mail or
    courier to the head office or place of business of the corporation
    or to the corporation's agent or mandatary;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} leaving a copy of it at the corporation's head
    office or place of business with an officer or other individual who
    appears to be in control of or to manage the head office or place of
    business or with the corporation's agent or mandatary; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy of it by electronic means, other
    than fax, to an individual referred to in paragraph (b).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A document may be served on a vessel by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} delivering a copy of it personally to the master or
    any other person who is, or appears to be, in charge of the vessel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fixing a copy of it to a prominent part of the
    vessel;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the vessel's authorized representative is an
    individual, sending a copy of it by fax, registered mail or courier
    to the authorized representative; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the vessel's authorized representative is a
    corporation,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy of it by fax, registered mail or
      courier to the authorized representative's head office or place of
      business,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} leaving a copy of it at the authorized
      representative's head office or place of business with an officer
      or other individual who appears to be in control of or to manage
      the head office or place of business or with the authorized
      representative's agent or mandatary, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy of it by electronic means, other
      than fax, to an individual referred to in subparagraph (ii).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A document that is served by registered mail is
  deemed to be served on the fourth day after the day on which it was
  mailed.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The following means are sufficient to prove service
  of a document:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a document transmitted by fax, a
    proof of transmission produced by the fax machine that sets out the
    date and time of transmission; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, an acknowledgment of service
    signed by or on behalf of the person served, specifying the date and
    place of service.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-246, s. 3(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20080403.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Notices]{.HTitleText1} {#h-748185 .Part}

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section applies in respect of the following notices:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice of refusal to issue referred to in
    subsection 16(5) of the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a notice of proposed suspension or cancellation
    referred to in section 20.1 of the Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a notice of suspension, cancellation or refusal to
    renew referred to in section 20.3 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice may be given to an individual

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} personally, by leaving a copy of it

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with the individual, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the individual cannot conveniently be found,
      with someone who appears to be an adult member of the same
      household at the last known address or usual place of residence of
      the individual; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by sending a copy of it by courier, fax or other
    electronic means to the last known address or usual place of
    residence of the individual.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A notice may be given to a corporation by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy of it by fax or courier to the head
    office or place of business of the corporation or to the
    corporation's agent or mandatary;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} leaving a copy of it at the corporation's head
    office or place of business with an officer or other individual who
    appears to be in control of or to manage the head office or place of
    business or with the corporation's agent or mandatary; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy of it by electronic means, other
    than fax, to an individual referred to in paragraph (b).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A notice may be given to a vessel by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} delivering a copy of it personally to the master or
    any other person who is, or appears to be, in charge of the vessel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fixing a copy of it to a prominent part of the
    vessel;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the vessel's authorized representative is an
    individual, sending a copy of it by fax or courier to the authorized
    representative; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the vessel's authorized representative is a
    corporation,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy of it by fax or courier to the
      authorized representative's head office or place of business,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} leaving a copy of it at the authorized
      representative's head office or place of business with an officer
      or other individual who appears to be in control of or to manage
      the head office or place of business or with the authorized
      representative's agent or mandatary, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} sending a copy of it by electronic means, other
      than fax, to an individual referred to in subparagraph (ii).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-246, s. 4
:::
